Method: 利用Weil限制和Weyl群作用，证明了一个关于仿射概形的通用结论，并通过Demazure算子构造坐标环。

Result: 给出了万有中心化子坐标环的显式描述，并建立了Weil限制的W-不动点概形的坐标环与Demazure算子之间的联系。

Conclusion: 在特定整性条件下，万有中心化子的坐标环可以通过Demazure算子从具有Weyl群作用的仿射概形的坐标环中提取出来。

Abstract: We give a simple description of the coordinate ring of the universal centralizer associated to a simply connected semisimple group. To this end, we prove a general result on Weil restriction of affine schemes $X$ over the Cartan subalgebra $\mathfrak{t}$ equipped with a compatible action of the Weyl group $W$. Specifically, we show that the coordinate ring of the scheme $\mathrm{Res}^W(X)$ of $W$-fixed points of Weil restriction of $X$ to the GIT quotient $\mathfrak{t}//W$ can be obtained from the coordinate ring of $X$ by applying Demazure operators if and only if the scheme $\mathrm{Res}^W(X)$ is integral.

</details>


<div id='stat.AP'></div>

# stat.AP [[Back]](#toc)

### [98] [On the use of satellite information to estimate agricultural carbon footprint in a small area framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25342)
*Riccardo Pajno,Felicetta Carillo,Paolo Maranzano,Timo Schmid,Riccardo Borgoni*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 本研究开发了一个基于模型的框架，整合调查、普查和卫星衍生排放数据，通过地统计学升尺度方法和参数自助法处理空间错位问题，为意大利波河流域的农业碳足迹提供可靠的小区域估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 农业部门因气候压力、人口结构变化和经济不均衡发展而快速变化，需要精细空间尺度的可靠环境指标，但有限的数据可用性常制约次区域分析。

Method: 将调查、普查和卫星衍生的氨排放数据整合到统一框架中；采用地统计学升尺度程序处理网格化卫星数据与行政边界之间的空间错位问题；结合参数自助法将协变量构建阶段的不确定性传播到最终小区域估计中。

Result: 卫星衍生信息显著提高了碳足迹估计的准确性和稳定性，同时减少了对大型、异质性辅助数据集的依赖。

Conclusion: 展示了地球观测数据在基于模型的环境统计中的潜力，为精细尺度农业环境监测提供了可行的方法论框架。

Abstract: The agricultural sector is undergoing rapid change due to climate pressures, demographic shifts, and uneven economic development, increasing the demand for reliable environmental indicators at fine spatial scales. However, limited data availability often constrains subregional analyses. This study develops a model-based framework for producing reliable small-area estimates for assessing the agricultural carbon footprint in the Po Valley (Northern Italy), a region characterized by intensive livestock farming and high environmental pressure. We integrate survey, census, and satellite-derived emission data into a unified framework and produce estimates at the level of Agrarian Subregions, defined as agriculturally homogeneous municipalities by the Italian National Institute of Statistics. Satellite-based ammonia emission data are incorporated as auxiliary covariates to improve precision and spatial coherence. A key methodological contribution is the treatment of spatial misalignment between gridded satellite data and administrative boundaries. This issue is addressed through a geostatistical upscaling procedure combined with a parametric bootstrap that propagates uncertainty from the covariate construction stage to the final small-area estimates. The results show that satellite-derived information substantially improves the accuracy and stability of carbon footprint estimates while reducing reliance on large, heterogeneous auxiliary datasets, illustrating the potential of Earth observation data in model-based environmental statistics.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [99] [VISION-SLS: Safe Perception-Based Control from Learned Visual Representations via System Level Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24894)
*Antoine P. Leeman,Shuyu Zhan,Melanie N. Zeilinger,Glen Chou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: VISION-SLS提出了一种基于高分辨率RGB图像的非线性输出反馈控制方法，通过预训练视觉特征学习低维观测映射并带有状态依赖误差界，结合系统级综合（SLS）优化因果仿射时变输出反馈策略，使用序列凸规划和Riccati递归求解非凸问题，在仿真和硬件实验中实现了带约束保证的鲁棒安全控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决高分辨率RGB图像下非线性输出反馈控制中存在的部分可观测性、传感器噪声和非线性动力学问题，同时保证约束满足，并提升方法在大规模场景下的可扩展性与实用性。

Method: (i) 从预训练视觉特征中学习低维观测映射并构建状态依赖误差界；(ii) 通过系统级综合优化因果仿射时变输出反馈策略；(iii) 利用序列凸规划和高效Riccati递归求解非凸优化问题。

Result: 在4D小车、10D四旋翼（≥512×512像素）、59D人形机器人（部分可观）仿真任务以及地面车辆硬件实验中，实现了安全、信息搜集行为，在安全率和求解时间上优于基线方法。

Conclusion: 学习的视觉抽象结合高效求解器使基于SLS的安全视觉运动输出反馈控制在大规模场景下变得实用可行。

Abstract: We propose VISION-SLS, a method for nonlinear output-feedback control from high-resolution RGB images which provides robust constraint satisfaction guarantees under calibrated uncertainty bounds despite partial observability, sensor noise, and nonlinear dynamics. To enable scalability while retaining guarantees, we propose: (i) a learned low-dimensional observation map from pretrained visual features with state-dependent error bounds, and (ii) a causal affine time-varying output-feedback policy optimized via System Level Synthesis (SLS). We develop a scalable, novel solver for the resulting nonconvex program that leverages sequential convex programming coupled with efficient Riccati recursions. On two simulated visuomotor tasks (a 4D car and a 10D quadrotor) with >= 512 x 512 pixels and a 59D humanoid task with partial observability, our method enables safe, information-gathering behavior that reduces uncertainty while guaranteeing constraint satisfaction with empirically-calibrated error bounds. We also validate our method on hardware, safely controlling a ground vehicle from onboard images, outperforming baselines in safety rate and solve times. Together, these results show that learned visual abstractions coupled with an efficient solver make SLS-based safe visuomotor output-feedback practical at scale. The code implementation of our method is available at https://github.com/trustworthyrobotics/VISION-SLS.

</details>


### [100] [An analysis of sensor selection for fruit picking with suction-based grippers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24906)
*Eva Krueger,Marcus Rosette,Joseph R. Davidson*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文针对机器人水果采摘中难以可靠检测采摘成功与否的问题，设计了一种集成多模态传感器的吸气式苹果夹爪，通过阶段依赖的传感策略，使用随机森林和多层感知机实现了超过90%的采摘状态分类准确率，并能提前预测即将发生的失败。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人水果采摘常因无法可靠检测果实是否成功采摘而导致效率低下和作物损伤增加；现有工作虽探索了视觉和传感学习方法，但最小传感器集和阶段依赖的采摘/滑移检测策略仍未被充分研究。

Method: 设计集成多模态传感器的吸气式苹果夹爪，在真实果园中进行阶段依赖的传感器评估，识别不同采摘阶段最具信息量的传感器组合，并使用随机森林和多层感知机分类器进行采摘状态分类。

Result: 随机森林和多层感知机分类器在检测成功采摘和即将发生的失败时准确率超过90%，随机森林预测采摘/滑移事件的时间与人工标注真值相差仅0.09秒。

Conclusion: 本文通过阶段依赖的多模态传感策略和最小传感器集识别，有效提升了机器人采摘状态估计的准确性和实时性，有望减少采摘失败和作物损伤。

Abstract: Robotic fruit harvesting often fails to reliably detect whether a fruit has been successfully picked, limiting efficiency and increasing crop damage. This problem is difficult due to compliant fruit and grippers, variable stem attachment, and occlusions in orchard environments. Prior work has explored vision-based perception and multi-sensor learning approaches for pick state estimation. However, minimal sensor sets and phase-dependent sensing strategies for accurate pick and slip detection remain largely unexplored. In this work, we design and evaluate a multimodal sensing suite integrated into a compliant suction-based apple gripper. Our approach is unique because it identifies which sensors are most informative at different phases of the pick, enabling predictive detection of failures before they occur. The contributions of this paper are a phase-dependent evaluation of multimodal sensors and the identification of minimal sensor sets for reliable pick state classification. Experiments in a real apple orchard show that Random Forest and Multilayer Perceptron classifiers detect successful picks and impending failures with over 90% accuracy, and Random Forest predicts pick/slip events within 0.09 s of human-annotated ground truth.

</details>


### [101] [asRoBallet: Closing the Sim2Real Gap via Friction-Aware Reinforcement Learning for Underactuated Spherical Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24916)
*Fang Wan,Guangyi Huang,Tianyu Wu,Zishang Zhang,Bangchao Huang,Haoran Sun,Mingdong Chen,Chaoyang Song*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了asRoBallet，首次成功将强化学习部署到人形球机器人硬件上，通过高保真仿真、摩擦感知RL框架和低成本硬件改造实现了零样本Sim2Real迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文献虽能使用LQR和MPC处理球机器人的3D平衡，但将RL迁移到真实人形球机器人硬件仍面临接触建模、执行器延迟与抖动、安全硬件探索等关键瓶颈。

Method: 1) 构建高保真MuJoCo仿真，显式建模ETH型全向轮的离散滚子力学，捕捉寄生振动和接触不连续；2) 提出摩擦感知RL框架，掌握轮-球和球-地界面的耦合摩擦通道，实现零样本Sim2Real迁移；3) 通过减法重构将过约束四足机器人的关键组件重新用于新设计的结构框架；4) 开发iOS生态系统将消费电子产品转变为低延迟接口。

Result: 首次在真实人形球机器人硬件上成功部署强化学习，实现了零样本从仿真到真实世界的迁移。

Conclusion: 通过高保真仿真建模和摩擦感知RL框架，有效弥合了Sim2Real差距，同时低成本的硬件改造和便捷的操作界面为该领域研究提供了可行的实验平台。

Abstract: We introduce asRoBallet, to the best of our knowledge, the first successful deployment of reinforcement learning (RL) on a humanoid ballbot hardware. Historically, ballbots have served as a canonical benchmark for underactuated and nonholonomic control, which are characterized by a reality gap in complex friction models for wheel-sphere-ground interactions. While current literature demonstrates successful handling of 3D balancing with LQR and MPC, transitioning to actual hardware for a humanoid ballbot using RL is currently hindered by critical gaps in contact modeling, actuator latency & jitter, and safe hardware exploration, and safe hardware exploration. This study proposes a high-fidelity MuJoCo simulation that explicitly models the discrete roller mechanics of ETH-type omni-wheels, thereby capturing parasitic vibrations and contact discontinuities that are previously ignored. We also developed a Friction-Aware Reinforcement Learning framework that achieves zero-shot Sim2Real transfer by mastering the coupled rolling, lateral, and torsional friction channels at the wheel-sphere and sphere-ground interfaces. We designed asRoBallet through subtractive reconfiguration, repurposing key components from an overconstrained quadruped and integrating them into a newly designed structural frame to achieve a robust research platform at low cost. We also developed a generalized iOS ecosystem that transforms consumer electronics into a low-latency interface, enabling a single operator to orchestrate expressive humanoid maneuvers via intuitive natural motion.

</details>


### [102] [Libra-VLA: Achieving Learning Equilibrium via Asynchronous Coarse-to-Fine Dual-System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24921)
*Yifei Wei,Linqing Zhong,Yi Liu,Yuxiang Lu,Xindong He,Maoqing Yao,Guanghui Ren*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Libra-VLA提出了一种粗到细的双系统VLA架构，通过将机器人操作层次分解为离散的宏方向到达和连续的微姿态对齐，实现异步执行策略，平衡了学习难度并弥合了语义-执行鸿沟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型采用单一生成范式，直接将视觉-语言特征映射到高频电机指令，忽视了机器人操作固有的层次结构，导致语义-执行鸿沟扩大，高层语义到连续动作的接地负担过重。

Method: 提出粗到细双系统架构：语义规划器（Semantic Planner）预测离散动作标记以捕捉宏方向意图，动作精炼器（Action Refiner）基于粗意图生成高频连续动作实现精确对齐，并采用异步执行策略。

Result: 性能与动作分解粒度呈倒U型曲线关系，当两个子系统的学习难度达到平衡时性能达到峰值；异步设计提供了可扩展、鲁棒且响应迅速的开放世界操作方案。

Conclusion: 通过显式解耦学习复杂度为粗到细的层次结构，并结合结构模块性实现异步执行策略，Libra-VLA为通用机器人操作提供了可扩展、鲁棒且高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models are a promising paradigm for generalist robotic manipulation by grounding high-level semantic instructions into executable physical actions. However, prevailing approaches typically adopt a monolithic generation paradigm, directly mapping visual-linguistic features to high-frequency motor commands in a flat, non-hierarchical fashion. This strategy overlooks the inherent hierarchy of robotic manipulation, where complex actions can be naturally modeled in a Hybrid Action Space, decomposing into discrete macro-directional reaching and continuous micro-pose alignment, severely widening the semantic-actuation gap and imposing a heavy representational burden on grounding high-level semantics to continuous actions. To address this, we introduce Libra-VLA, a novel Coarse-to-Fine Dual-System VLA architecture. We explicitly decouple the learning complexity into a coarse-to-fine hierarchy to strike a training equilibrium, while simultaneously leveraging this structural modularity to implement an asynchronous execution strategy. The Semantic Planner predicts discrete action tokens capturing macro-directional intent, while the Action Refiner conditions on coarse intent to generate high-frequency continuous actions for precise alignment. Crucially, our empirical analysis reveals that performance follows an inverted-U curve relative to action decomposition granularity, peaking exactly when the learning difficulty is balanced between the two sub-systems. With the asynchronous design, our approach offers a scalable, robust, and responsive solution for open-world manipulation.

</details>


### [103] [TEACar: An Open-Source Autonomous Driving Platform](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24934)
*Zhongzheng Zhang,Maxwell Ruyle,Andrew Kappes,Tyler Ruble,William Shaoul,Dana Moreno,Jack Penn,Ivan Ruchkin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出TEACAR，一个1/14至1/16比例的模块化自动驾驶平台，采用四层甲板结构解耦感知、计算、驱动与电源子系统，基于ROS 2软件架构，通过三个CNN转向控制器验证其机械稳定性、计算性能与可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有小型自动驾驶赛车平台存在模块化不足、集成复杂度高、可扩展性受限等问题，无法有效支持ITS研究中基于视觉感知和学习控制的硬件在环验证需求。

Method: 设计四层甲板机械结构实现物理解耦（感知/计算/驱动/电源），采用硬件抽象层与ROS 2软件架构，构建1/14-1/16比例原型平台，使用三个CNN转向控制器进行综合性能评估。

Result: 实验量化了平台的机械稳定性、结构特性与软件性能，包括推理延迟、功耗和系统运行时间等指标，证明了TEACAR作为可扩展、模块化、成本有效的ITS研究测试平台的有效性。

Conclusion: TEACAR提供了一种模块化、可扩展且经济实惠的小型自动驾驶测试平台方案，适用于ITS研究、教育及开发，相关代码已开源在GitHub。

Abstract: Intelligent Transportation Systems (ITS) increasingly rely on vision-based perception and learning-based control, necessitating experimental platforms that support realistic hardware-in-the-loop validation. Small-scale platforms for autonomous racing offer a practical path to hardware validation, but often suffer from limited modularity, high integration complexity, or restricted extensibility. This paper presents TEACAR, a 1/14- to 1/16-scale autonomous driving platform designed with modular mechanical architecture, hardware abstraction, and ROS 2-based software. The system adopts a four-layer deck structure that physically decouples sensing, computation, actuation, and power subsystems, improving structural rigidity while simplifying reconfiguration. We constructed and comprehensively evaluated the prototype of TEACAR. Its mechanical stability, structural characteristics, and software performance were quantified based on three CNN-based steering controllers. Inference latency, power consumption, and system operating time were measured to evaluate computational capability and robustness. Our experiments demonstrated that TEACAR offers a scalable, modular, and cost-effective testbed for ITS research, education, and development. Our project repository is available on GitHub.

</details>


### [104] [DiscreteRTC: Discrete Diffusion Policies are Natural Asynchronous Executors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25050)
*Pengcheng Wang,Kaiwen Hong,Chensheng Peng,Katherine Driggs-Campbell,Masayoshi Tomizuka,Chenfeng Xu,Chen Tang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出DiscreteRTC，利用离散扩散策略的原生掩码修复能力替代流匹配策略的异步修复方案，在物理AI的动态任务中实现更高效、更简单的异步执行，无需微调且推理计算量更低，在模拟和真实动态操作任务中成功率提升50%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理AI（如机器人）需要在环境持续变化时实时行动，同步执行器的帧间停顿即使推理速度再快也会导致动态任务失败。异步执行（边思考边行动）是结构性需求，现有基于流匹配策略的实时分块（RTC）虽通过修复机制实现异步，但存在结构次优问题：其修复依赖推理时修正而非基础策略，导致几乎没有预训练收益、需要特定微调、启发式引导和额外计算增加延迟。

Method: 观察到离散扩散策略通过迭代去掩码生成动作，天然具备异步执行能力，其原生操作就是修复（inpainting），无需微调；早期停止（early stopping）提供自适应引导并降低推理成本。提出DiscreteRTC，用原生去掩码替代外部修正。

Result: 在动态模拟基准和真实世界动态操作任务上，DiscreteRTC相比基于流匹配的RTC及其他基线获得更高成功率。具体地：实现异步修复无需额外代码（0行），推理计算量仅为从头生成动作的0.7倍，在真实动态抓取任务中成功率提升50%。

Conclusion: DiscreteRTC更简单（无需微调实现异步修复）、更快（推理计算量减少30%）、更优（成功率显著提升），是物理AI异步执行的有效框架。

Abstract: Unlike chatbots, physical AI must act while the world keeps evolving. Therefore, the inter-chunk pause of synchronous executors are fatal for dynamic tasks regardless of how fast the inference is. Asynchronous execution -- thinking while acting -- is therefore a structural requirement, and real-time chunking (RTC) makes it viable by recasting chunk transitions as inpainting: freezing committed actions and consistently generating the remainder. However, RTC with flow-matching policy is structurally suboptimal: its inpainting comes from inference-time corrections rather than the base policy, yielding little pre-training benefit, specific fine-tuning, heuristic guidance, and extra computation that inflates the latency. In this work, we observe that discrete diffusion policies, which generate actions by iteratively unmasking, are natural asynchronous executors that resolve all limitations at once: they are fine-tuning free since inpainting is their native operation, while early stopping further provides adaptive guidance and reduces inference cost. We propose DiscreteRTC, which replaces external corrections with native unmasking, and show on dynamic simulated benchmarks and real-world dynamic manipulation tasks that it achieves higher success rates than continuous RTC and other baselines. In summary, DiscreteRTC is simpler to implement with 0 lines of code for async inpainting, faster at inference with only 0.7x computation compared with generating actions from scratch, and better at execution with 50% higher success rate in real-world dynamic pick task compared with flow-matching-based RTC. More visualizations are on https://outsider86.github.io/DiscreteRTCSite/.

</details>


### [105] [HANDFUL: Sequential Grasp-Conditioned Dexterous Manipulation with Resource Awareness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25126)
*Ethan Foong,Yunshuang Li,Hao Jiang,Gaurav S. Sukhatme,Daniel Seita*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出HANDFUL框架，通过资源感知抓取规划（将手指使用视为有限资源）解决灵巧手在多技能顺序操作中的问题，在仿真和真实机器人上验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有灵巧操作研究大多聚焦于单物体、单技能任务，而实际中机器人需要顺序执行多个操作技能，同时保持对之前抓取物体的控制。如何在学习初始抓取时考虑后续任务对手指资源的约束是一个关键但未被充分研究的问题。

Method: 提出HANDFUL框架，包含：(1) 通过手指级接触奖励鼓励探索资源感知抓取（保留可用手指）；(2) 基于课程学习的策略选择适合下游任务的抓取方式；并构建HANDFUL-Bench仿真基准（包含推、拉、按等第二子任务）。

Result: 在仿真实验中，优先学习资源感知抓取相比贪婪优化初始抓取的基线方法，显著提升了第二子任务的成功率和鲁棒性；并在真实LEAP灵巧手上验证了方法的可迁移性。

Conclusion: 资源感知抓取规划是多功能灵巧操作的关键原则，本文为顺序条件化灵巧操作任务提供了有效的学习框架和基准。

Abstract: Dexterous robot hands offer rich opportunities for multifunctional manipulation, where a robot must execute multiple skills in sequence while maintaining control over previously grasped objects. Most prior work in dexterous manipulation focuses on single-object, single-skill tasks. In contrast, our insight is that many sequential tasks require resource-aware grasps that conserve fingers for future actions. In this paper, we study sequential grasp-conditioned dexterous manipulation, where a robot first grasps an object and then performs a second, distinct manipulation subtask while preserving the initial grasp. We introduce HANDFUL, a learning framework that models finger usage as a limited resource and encourages exploration of resource-aware grasps through finger-level contact rewards. These grasps are subsequently selected for downstream tasks via curriculum-based policy learning. We further propose HANDFUL-Bench, a simulation benchmark that introduces sequential dexterous manipulation tasks across multiple secondsubtask objectives, including pushing, pulling, and pressing, under a shared grasp-conditioned setup. Extensive simulation results demonstrate that prioritizing resource-aware grasps improves second-subtask success and robustness compared to a baseline that greedily optimizes the initial grasp before attempting the second subtask. We additionally validate our approach on a real dexterous LEAP hand. Together, this work establishes resource-aware grasp planning as a key principle for multifunctional dexterous manipulation. Supplementary material is available on our website: https://handful-dex.github.io.

</details>


### [106] [Dynamic UGV-UAV Cooperative Path Planning in Uncertain Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25267)
*Ninh Nguyen,Srinivas Akella*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究了动态UGV-UAV协同路径规划（DUCPP）问题，提出包括双向策略在内的多种方法，在不确定路网中通过UAV动态检测不可通行边来优化UGV的安全路径。实验表明双向策略性能最优，多UAV可减少UGV行程时间但增加计算开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在灾害响应、紧急物资运输和救援等场景中，地面车辆（UGV）需在道路条件部分未知的不确定路网中安全高效到达目的地，现有方法难以有效处理动态不确定环境下的UGV-UAV协同路径规划问题。

Method: 提出了多种UGV-UAV协同策略，包括双向搜索策略，利用UAV动态检测路网中的边以识别和剔除损坏或不可通行的边，并探究了多UAV对UGV行程时间及计算时间的影响。

Result: 在100个城市路网上的实验表明：双向策略在多数实例中表现最佳；使用多UAV可进一步减少UGV行程时间，但会增加计算时间开销。

Conclusion: 本文为DUCPP问题提供了一个鲁棒的框架，实现了高效的UGV-UAV协同路径规划与道路检测，为不确定环境下的导航提供了实用解决方案。

Abstract: This paper addresses the Dynamic UGV-UAV Cooperative Path Planning (DUCPP) problem involving one unmanned ground vehicle (UGV) assisted by one or more unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs) operating on an uncertain road network with potentially impassable edges. DUCPP is particularly relevant for scenarios such as disaster response, emergency supply transport, and rescue operations, where a UGV must reach a specified destination in the presence of partially unknown road conditions. To enable the UGV to travel safely and efficiently to its destination, the UAV(s) dynamically inspect edges in the environment to identify and prune damaged or impassable edges from consideration.
  We present multiple strategies, including a bidirectional approach, to optimize UGV-UAV cooperation for finding a safe path in an uncertain road network. Furthermore, we explore the impact of using multiple UAVs on reducing the UGV's travel time, and evaluate the associated computation time. The proposed strategies are implemented and evaluated on 100 urban road networks. The results demonstrate that the bidirectional strategy achieves the best performance in most instances, and using multiple UAVs further reduces UGV travel time at the expense of increased computation time. This paper presents a robust framework for DUCPP to achieve efficient UGV-UAV cooperation for path planning and inspection, offering practical solutions for navigation in challenging and uncertain conditions.

</details>


### [107] [Slot-hopping Enabled Loiter Guidance and Automation for Fixed-wing UAV Corridors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25292)
*Pradeep J,Siddhardha Kedarisetty,Ashwini Ratnoo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种半协同制导策略，通过在固定翼无人机走廊的徘徊车道中实现最小干扰的插入和紧凑编排，解决交通拥堵管理问题。策略包括：优先尝试在速度限制内与空闲时隙交会；若不可行，则协调少量徘徊无人机进行时隙跳跃以创建可用时隙。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 固定翼无人机走廊中的交通拥堵管理问题亟需解决，现有徘徊车道框架在插入无人机时会对已运行无人机造成较大干扰，且无法实现紧凑的走廊编排。

Method: 提出半协同制导策略：首先尝试在速度限制内让进入的无人机与现有空闲时隙交会；若直接插入不可行，则协调最小数量的徘徊无人机执行协同时隙跳跃，以创建一个位置合适的空时隙。

Result: 通过数值仿真验证了该方法的可行性和性能。

Conclusion: 所提出的半协同策略能在最小干扰下实现固定翼无人机向徘徊车道的插入，并支持更紧凑的走廊布局，有效缓解交通拥堵。

Abstract: This paper addresses the problem of traffic congestion management in fixed-wing unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV) corridors by further developing a recently introduced loiter-lane framework. A semi-cooperative guidance strategy is developed for inserting fixed-wing UAVs into a loiter lane with minimal disruption to the UAVs already operating within it, while enabling a more compact fixed-wing UAV corridor. Building on the concepts of cooperative and non-disruptive loiter-lane insertion, the proposed strategy makes the incoming UAV first attempt, within its speed bounds, to rendezvous with an existing empty loiter slot. If direct insertion is infeasible, a minimal number of loitering UAVs perform coordinated slot hopping to create a suitably positioned empty slot. The feasibility and performance of the method are demonstrated through numerical simulations.

</details>


### [108] [ANCHOR: A Physically Grounded Closed-Loop Framework for Robust Home-Service Mobile Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25323)
*Jinhao Jiang,Shengyu Fang,Sibo Zuo,Yujie Tang,Yirui Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ANCHOR是一个物理锚定的闭环框架，通过绑定符号谓词与可观测几何锚点、可操作性感知的基座对齐以及最小责任层分层恢复，解决了移动操作中符号计划与物理世界不一致的问题，在60次真实机器人实验中成功率达71.7%，扰动下恢复率71.4%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有开放词汇移动操作系统在长期任务执行中因符号计划与物理世界的不一致性而频繁失败，具体表现为依赖预扫描语义地图导致场景变化后不一致、导航终点不考虑下游操作可行性造成“已到达但无法操作”、以及通过无差异全局重规划处理异常导致局部错误扩散。

Method: 提出了三个机制：(i) 物理锚定任务规划，将符号谓词绑定到可观测几何锚点并在每个动作后重新验证；(ii) 可操作性感知基座对齐，确保导航终点满足运动学可达性和局部碰撞可行性；(iii) 最小责任层分层恢复，在感知、基座-手臂协调和执行层定位失败以防止级联重试。

Result: 在60次真实机器人实验中，任务成功率从53.3%提升至71.7%，扰动下恢复率达71.4%。

Conclusion: 明确的物理锚定和结构化失败隔离对于鲁棒的移动操作至关重要。

Abstract: Recent advances in open-vocabulary mobile manipulation have brought robots into real domestic environments. In such settings, reliable long-horizon execution under open-set object references and frequent disturbances becomes essential. However, many failures persist. These are not caused by semantic misunderstanding but by inconsistencies between symbolic plans and the evolving physical world, manifested as three recurring limitations: (i) existing systems often rely on pre-scanned semantic maps that become inconsistent after scene changes and disturbances; (ii) they select navigation endpoints without considering downstream manipulation feasibility, causing the "arrived but inoperable" problem; and (iii) they handle anomalies through undifferentiated global replanning, which often fails to contain local errors. To address this execution inconsistency, we present ANCHOR, a physically grounded closed-loop framework that aligns symbolic reasoning with verifiable physical state during execution. ANCHOR integrates three mechanisms: (i) physically anchored task planning, which binds symbolic predicates to observable geometric anchors and re-validates them after each action; (ii) operability-aware base alignment, which ensures that navigation endpoints satisfy kinematic reachability and local collision feasibility; and (iii) minimum-responsible-layer hierarchical recovery, which localizes failures across perception, base-arm coordination, and execution layers to prevent cascading retries. Across 60 real-robot trials in previously unseen environments, ANCHOR improves task success from 53.3% to 71.7% and achieves a 71.4% recovery rate under perturbations, demonstrating that explicit physical grounding and structured failure containment are critical for robust mobile manipulation. Our project page is available at https://anchor9178.github.io/ANCHOR/ .

</details>


### [109] [ProDrive: Proactive Planning for Autonomous Driving via Ego-Environment Co-Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25329)
*Chuyao Fu,Shengzhe Gan,Zhuoli Ouyang,Yuhan Rui,Xiaowei Chi,Sirui Han,Jiankun Wang,Hong Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ProDrive提出了一种基于世界模型的主动规划框架，通过联合训练查询中心轨迹规划器与BEV世界模型，实现自车-环境的协同演化，在NAVSIM v1上超越强基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有端到端自动驾驶规划器仅依赖当前观测生成轨迹，无法预测未来场景演化，导致短视决策和安全性问题。

Method: 联合训练查询中心轨迹规划器（生成多样化候选轨迹和规划感知的自车token）与BEV世界模型（预测未来场景演化），通过将规划器特征注入世界模型并并行评估所有候选轨迹，保持端到端梯度流动。

Result: 在NAVSIM v1上，ProDrive在安全性和规划效率方面均优于强基线方法。

Conclusion: 双向耦合的自车-环境协同设计能够实现超越当前观测驱动的主动规划，消融实验验证了该设计的有效性。

Abstract: End-to-end autonomous driving planners typically generate trajectories from current observations alone. However, real-world driving is highly dynamic, and such reactive planning cannot anticipate future scene evolution, often leading to myopic decisions and safety-critical failures. We propose ProDrive, a world-model-based proactive planning framework that enables ego-environment co-evolution for autonomous driving. ProDrive jointly trains a query-centric trajectory planner and a bird's-eye-view (BEV) world model end-to-end: the planner generates diverse candidate trajectories and planning-aware ego tokens, while the world model predicts future scene evolution conditioned on them. By injecting planner features into the world model and evaluating all candidates in parallel, ProDrive preserves end-to-end gradient flow and allows future outcome assessment to directly shape planning. This bidirectional coupling enables proactive planning beyond current-observation-driven decision-making. Experiments on NAVSIM v1 show that ProDrive outperforms strong baselines in both safety and planning efficiency, while ablations validate the effectiveness of the proposed ego-environment coupling design.

</details>


### [110] [Robust Graph Matching through Semantic Relationship Generation for SLAM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25404)
*David Perez-Saura,Jose Andres Millan-Romera,Miguel Fernandez-Cortizas,Holger Voos,Pascual Campoy,Jose Luis Sanchez-Lopez*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种语义增强的图匹配方法，通过建模检测对象与结构元素（房间、墙面）之间的关系，在具有重复或对称布局的室内环境中实现更鲁棒和高效的全局定位。该方法在iS-Graphs框架下集成，显著减少了候选匹配数量，提高了计算效率，尤其在纯几何方法失效的对称场景中表现优越。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在结构化室内环境中，基于场景图的定位方法在面对重复性或对称性布局时，仅依赖结构线索往往不足以消除歧义，导致匹配失败或计算开销过大。

Method: 从RGB-D数据中检测物体并将其集成到图中，显式建模物体与结构元素（房间、墙面）之间的语义关系，利用这些关系在几何验证前过滤候选对应关系，从而降低歧义性和搜索复杂度。该方法集成在iS-Graphs框架内。

Result: 在合成和模拟环境中的实验表明，语义关系显著减少了候选匹配数量，提升了计算效率，并实现了更快的收敛，尤其是在纯几何方法失效的对称场景中。

Conclusion: 语义增强的图匹配方法能有效解决对称/重复布局下的定位歧义问题，在提升鲁棒性的同时降低计算成本。

Abstract: Graph-based representations such as Scene Graphs enable localization in structured indoor environments by matching a locally observed graph, constructed from sensor data, to a prior map. This process is particularly challenging in environments with repetitive or symmetric layouts, where structural cues alone are often insufficient to resolve ambiguities. We propose a semantic-enhanced graph matching approach that explicitly models relations between detected objects and structural elements, such as rooms and wall planes. Objects are detected from RGB-D data and integrated into the graph, and their relations to structural elements are exploited to filter candidate correspondences prior to geometric verification, significantly reducing ambiguity and search complexity. The proposed method is integrated within the iS-Graphs framework and evaluated in synthetic and simulated environments. Results show that semantic relations significantly reduce the number of candidate matches, improve computational efficiency, and enable faster convergence, particularly in symmetric scenarios where purely geometric approaches fail.

</details>


### [111] [GS-Playground: A High-Throughput Photorealistic Simulator for Vision-Informed Robot Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25459)
*Yufei Jia,Heng Zhang,Ziheng Zhang,Junzhe Wu,Mingrui Yu,Zifan Wang,Dixuan Jiang,Zheng Li,Chenyu Cao,Zhuoyuan Yu,Xun Yang,Haizhou Ge,Yuchi Zhang,Jiayuan Zhang,Zhenbiao Huang,Tianle Liu,Shenyu Chen,Jiacheng Wang,Bin Xie,Xuran Yao,Xiwa Deng,Guangyu Wang,Jinzhi Zhang,Lei Hao,Zhixing Chen,Yuxiang Chen,Anqi Wang,Hongyun Tian,Yiyi Yan,Zhanxiang Cao,Yizhou Jiang,Hanyang Shao,Yue Li,Lu Shi,Bokui Chen,Wei Sui,Hanqing Cui,Yusen Qin,Ruqi Huang,Lei Han,Tiancai Wang,Guyue Zhou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: GS-Playground是一个多模态仿真框架，通过将批量3D高斯泼溅渲染与高性能并行物理引擎集成，实现10^4 FPS（640×480分辨率）的突破性吞吐量，并引入自动化Real2Sim工作流，有效弥合了具身AI中视觉感知与物理交互之间的鸿沟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大规模并行仿真器在基于本体感知的 locomotion 任务中取得了突破，但由于大规模逼真渲染的计算开销过高，其潜力在视觉感知任务中未得到充分利用。同时，仿真就绪3D资产的创建依赖人工建模，且sim-to-real物理差距阻碍了接触密集型操作策略的迁移。

Method: 提出GS-Playground框架：(1) 设计高性能并行物理引擎，与批量3DGS渲染管线集成，实现高保真同步；(2) 提出自动Real2Sim流程，重建逼真、物理一致且内存高效的环境；(3) 系统达到10^4 FPS（640×480分辨率）的吞吐量。

Result: 在 locomotion、navigation 和 manipulation 等多类具身任务中，GS-Playground有效弥合了感知和物理差距，实现了大规模视觉强化学习的高效训练。

Conclusion: GS-Playground通过高性能并行渲染-物理仿真集成及自动场景生成，显著降低了大规模视觉RL的门槛，为视觉中心具身AI研究提供了高效平台。

Abstract: Embodied AI research is undergoing a shift toward vision-centric perceptual paradigms. While massively parallel simulators have catalyzed breakthroughs in proprioception-based locomotion, their potential remains largely untapped for vision-informed tasks due to the prohibitive computational overhead of large-scale photorealistic rendering. Furthermore, the creation of simulation-ready 3D assets heavily relies on labor-intensive manual modeling, while the significant sim-to-real physical gap hinders the transfer of contact-rich manipulation policies. To address these bottlenecks, we propose GS-Playground, a multi-modal simulation framework designed to accelerate end-to-end perceptual learning. We develop a novel high-performance parallel physics engine, specifically designed to integrate with a batch 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) rendering pipeline to ensure high-fidelity synchronization. Our system achieves a breakthrough throughput of 10^4 FPS at 640x480 resolution, significantly lowering the barrier for large-scale visual RL. Additionally, we introduce an automated Real2Sim workflow that reconstructs photorealistic, physically consistent, and memory-efficient environments, streamlining the generation of complex simulation-ready scenes. Extensive experiments on locomotion, navigation, and manipulation demonstrate that GS-Playground effectively bridges the perceptual and physical gaps across diverse embodied tasks. Project homepage: https://gsplayground.github.io.

</details>


### [112] [Egocentric Tactile and Proximity Sensors as Observation Priors for Humanoid Collision Avoidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25554)
*Carson Kohlbrenner,Niraj Pudasaini,William Xie,Naren Sivagnanadasan,Nikolaus Correll,Alessandro Roncone*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一个强化学习框架，用于人形机器人H1-2的全身避碰，并通过躲避球任务分析传感器特性（覆盖范围、类型、距离）对避障行为的影响。研究发现：原始近距测量可替代显式物体定位（前提是感知范围足够）；稀疏非定向近距信号在样本效率上优于密集定向信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 触觉和近距传感器因抗遮挡性优于外部摄像头而被广泛用于机器人避碰，但传感器的特性（覆盖范围、类型、距离）如何影响避障行为尚不明确。

Method: 提出一个强化学习框架，以躲避球为基准任务，对分布在人形机器人H1-2上半身的传感器特性进行消融实验，对比不同传感范围、类型（定向/非定向）和密度下的避碰表现。

Result: 1) 原始近距测量在感知范围足够时可替代显式物体定位；2) 稀疏非定向近距信号在样本效率上优于密集定向信号。

Conclusion: 传感器特性的选择对学习避碰行为至关重要，非定向稀疏近距传感方案兼具效率和效果优势。

Abstract: Collision-free motion is often aided by tactile and proximity sensors distributed on the body of the robot due to their resistance to occlusion as opposed to external cameras. However, how to shape the sensor's properties, such as sensing coverage; type; and range, to enable avoidant behavior remains unclear. In this work, we present a reinforcement learning framework for whole-body collision avoidance on a humanoid H1-2 robot and use it to characterize how sensor properties shape learned avoidance behavior. Using dodgeball as a benchmark task, we ablate the properties of sensors distributed across the upper body of the robot and find that raw proximity measurements can substitute for explicit object localization provided the sensing range is sufficient and that sparse non-directional proximity signals outpace dense directional alternatives in sample efficiency.

</details>


### [113] [Improving Sensing Coverage and Compliance of 3D-Printed Artificial Skins Through Multi-Modal Sensing and Soft Materials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25563)
*Carson Kohlbrenner,Caleb Escobedo,Sayak Ray,Alexander Dickhans,Anna Soukhovei,Nickolaus Jackoski,Lyle Antieau,Alessandro Roncone*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种3D打印人工皮肤，通过混合飞行时间（ToF）和自电容（SC）传感模式，实现了多模态传感集成、柔软覆盖层用于冲击吸收和压力传感，以及简化的电气接口。在FR3机械臂上部署6个单元共40个传感元件，展示了接触检测、场景重建和压力相关触觉响应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D打印人工皮肤存在两大局限：单模态传感（仅能检测单一类型信号）和刚性材料（缺乏柔顺性，无法吸收冲击）。为提升实际可用性，需要多模态集成和柔软覆盖层。

Method: 采用混合ToF（飞行时间）和SC（自电容）传感方案，结合柔软覆盖层实现多模态感知。通过3D打印制备导电走线并设计简化的电气接口连接外部电子设备。

Result: 在FR3机械臂上部署6个人工皮肤单元（40个传感元件），成功实现接触检测、场景重建以及带柔软覆盖层的压力相关触觉响应。

Conclusion: 混合ToF和SC传感模式的3D打印人工皮肤能够同时实现接触检测、场景重建和压力触觉响应，且具备柔软覆盖层和简化的电气接口，提升了实际应用可行性。

Abstract: 3D-printed artificial skins are a scalable approach to whole-body tactile and proximity coverage, but prior implementations have been limited to unimodal sensing and rigid materials. To improve the practical usability of 3D-printed artificial skins, we present a hybrid time-of-flight (ToF) and self-capacitance (SC) sensing skin that demonstrates multi-modal sensing integration, soft compliant coverings for impact absorption and pressure sensing, and a streamlined electrical interface between printed conductive traces and external electronics. We show that combining ToF and SC modalities enables contact detection, scene reconstruction, and pressure-correlated tactile responses with the compliant covering by deploying six artificial skin units with 40 sensing elements over an FR3 robot arm.

</details>


### [114] [SlicerRoboTMS: An Open-Source 3D Slicer Extension for Robot-Assisted Transcranial Magnetic Stimulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25661)
*Wenzhi Bai,Yituo Guo,Bhaskar Basu,Andrew Weightman,Zhenhong Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了SlicerRoboTMS，一个基于3D Slicer的开源扩展，为机器人辅助经颅磁刺激（Robo-TMS）研究提供统一交互基础设施，支持MRI引导的神经导航和机器人系统集成，降低了多学科交叉的研究门槛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Robo-TMS开发需要医学影像、计算机视觉和机器人学等多学科专业知识，现有工具缺乏统一平台，开发门槛高。

Method: 基于3D Slicer开发扩展模块，支持MRI神经导航，通过标准化通信协议和可配置系统描述与机器人系统交互。

Result: 实现了SlicerRoboTMS开源扩展，并通过一个代表性工作流示例验证了其集成可行性。

Conclusion: SlicerRoboTMS降低了Robo-TMS研究门槛，支持多样化硬件配置和快速原型开发，促进了可重复和可扩展的研究。

Abstract: Robot-assisted Transcranial Magnetic Stimulation (Robo-TMS) is an image-guided robotic intervention that enhances the accuracy and reproducibility of conventional Transcranial Magnetic Stimulation (TMS), a widely used non-invasive brain stimulation procedure in clinical treatment and neuroscience research. Despite its potential, the development of Robo-TMS remains challenging due to the need for multidisciplinary expertise spanning medical imaging, computer vision, and robotics. This paper presents SlicerRoboTMS, an open-source 3D Slicer extension that provides a unified interaction infrastructure for Robo-TMS research. By leveraging 3D Slicer's medical image computing and visualisation capabilities, the extension supports Magnetic Resonance Imaging (MRI)-based neuronavigation and interfaces with robotic systems through standardised communication protocols and configurable system descriptions. An example integration is presented to demonstrate how SlicerRoboTMS can be incorporated into a representative Robo-TMS workflow. Designed to support diverse hardware configurations and rapid prototyping, SlicerRoboTMS lowers the barrier to entry and facilitates reproducible and extensible research in Robo-TMS. The extension is available at https://github.com/OpenRoboTMS/SlicerRoboTMS.

</details>


### [115] [GEGLU-Transformer for IMU-to-EMG Estimation with Few-Shot Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25670)
*Miroljub Mihailovic,Luca Tonin,Stefano Tortora,Emanuele Menegatti*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于GEGLU-Transformer的自适应IMU-to-EMG学习框架，通过惯性测量单元信号重建肌肉激活包络，在跨受试者场景下仅需0.5%的少量适应数据即可实现高精度肌肉激活估计，为可穿戴机器人提供替代直接EMG传感的实用方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表面肌电信号在实验室外环境中因电极敏感性、信号非平稳性和强受试者依赖性难以稳健部署，亟需一种鲁棒且可扩展的神经肌肉激活估计方法，以支持可穿戴机器人的自适应与个性化控制。

Method: 提出GEGLU-Transformer框架，结合Transformer编码器与高斯误差门控线性单元构建IMU-to-EMG映射模型；采用严格留一受试者交叉验证，并在多条件下肢生物力学数据集上评估，仅用0.5%的目标受试者数据进行轻量级适应。

Result: 无个体适应时皮尔逊相关系数r=0.706±0.139、R²=0.474±0.208；使用0.5%适应数据后r提升至0.761±0.030、R²=0.559±0.047，呈现快速适应和早期性能饱和特征。

Conclusion: 基于注意力机制的架构结合轻量级适应策略，可作为直接EMG传感的实用且可扩展替代方案，适用于真实世界可穿戴机器人应用。

Abstract: Reliable estimation of neuromuscular activation is a key enabler for adaptive and personalized control in wearable robotics. However, surface electromyography (EMG) remains difficult to deploy robustly outside laboratory settings due to electrode sensitivity, signal non-stationarity, and strong subject dependence. In this work, we propose an adaptive IMU-to-EMG learning framework that reconstructs continuous muscle activation envelopes from wearable inertial measurements across heterogeneous movement conditions. The approach combines a Transformer encoder with Gaussian Error Gated Linear Units (GEGLU-Transformer) to enhance cross-subject generalization and enable rapid subject-specific personalization. Under a strict leave-one-subject-out (LOSO) protocol on a multi-condition lower-limb biomechanics dataset, the proposed architecture achieves r = 0.706 +/- 0.139 and R^2 = 0.474 +/- 0.208 without subject-specific adaptation. With only 0.5% adaptation data, performance increases to r = 0.761 +/- 0.030 and R^2 = 0.559 +/- 0.047, demonstrating rapid adaptation and early performance saturation. These results support attention-based architectures combined with lightweight adaptation as a practical and scalable alternative to direct EMG sensing for real-world wearable robotic applications.

</details>


### [116] [Sensitivity-Based Tube NMPC for Cooperative Aerial Structures Under Parametric Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25766)
*Giuseppe Silano,Quentin Sablé,Marco Tognon,Luigi Iannelli,Antonio Franchi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于灵敏度的管式非线性模型预测控制（NMPC）框架，用于解决有界参数不确定性下的协作式空中链式结构的鲁棒控制问题。通过在水平线上传播一阶参数状态灵敏度并在线计算约束收紧裕度，实现了对连杆质量、长度和惯性不确定性的鲁棒性。MATLAB仿真和蒙特卡洛采样验证了该方法在保证约束边界的同时，跟踪性能与名义NMPC相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 协作式空中链式结构（如多飞行器通过刚性连杆连接）在实际应用中面临参数不确定性（如连杆质量、长度、惯性），这些不确定性会影响系统的安全性和约束满足性，需要一种鲁棒控制方法。

Method: 提出灵敏度-based 管式NMPC框架：考虑平面两车链式结构，采用输入速率驱动以限制推力和扭矩的转换速率与幅度；通过沿预测水平线传播一阶参数状态灵敏度，在线计算约束收紧裕度；使用平滑余弦嵌入实现链间分离约束的鲁棒化，并对推力幅度边界进行鲁棒化处理。

Result: 在边界贴近机动和蒙特卡洛不确定性采样下，该方法在不确定性条件下改善了约束裕度，跟踪性能与名义NMPC相当。

Conclusion: 基于灵敏度的管式NMPC能有效处理空中链式结构中的有界参数不确定性，在不显著牺牲跟踪性能的前提下提升约束鲁棒性。

Abstract: This paper presents a sensitivity-based tube Nonlinear Model Predictive Control (NMPC) framework for cooperative aerial chains under bounded parametric uncertainty. We consider a planar two-vehicle chain connected by rigid links, modeled with input-rate actuation to enforce slew-rate and magnitude limits on thrust and torque. Robustness to uncertainty in link mass, length, and inertia is achieved by propagating first-order parametric state sensitivities along the horizon and using them to compute online constraint-tightening margins. We robustify an inter-link separation constraint, implemented via a smooth cosine embedding, and thrust-magnitude bounds. The method is implemented in MATLAB and evaluated with boundary-hugging maneuvers and Monte-Carlo uncertainty sampling. Results show improved constraint margins under uncertainty with tracking performance comparable to nominal NMPC.

</details>


### [117] [KinDER: A Physical Reasoning Benchmark for Robot Learning and Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25788)
*Yixuan Huang,Bowen Li,Vaibhav Saxena,Yichao Liang,Utkarsh Aashu Mishra,Liang Ji,Lihan Zha,Jimmy Wu,Nishanth Kumar,Sebastian Scherer,Danfei Xu,Tom Silver*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: KinDER是一个针对机器人运动学和动力学具身推理的基准测试平台，包含25个程序生成的环境、参数化技能库和13个基线方法。实验表明现有方法在物理推理任务上表现不佳，并提供了真实-仿真-真实实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人系统需要推理自身、环境和任务带来的运动学与动力学约束，但现有方法在物理推理能力上存在明显不足，缺乏系统性评估基准。

Method: 构建了25个程序化生成的环境，设计了五个核心物理推理挑战（空间关系、非抓取多物体操作、工具使用、组合几何约束、动态约束），提供了Gymnasium兼容的Python库和13种基线方法（涵盖任务与运动规划、模仿学习、强化学习、基础模型方法），并进行了真实-仿真-真实实验。

Result: 现有方法在多个环境上表现困难，揭示了当前物理推理方法存在显著差距。

Conclusion: KinDER为机器人物理推理提供了系统性比较平台，有助于推动该领域发展。

Abstract: Robotic systems that interact with the physical world must reason about kinematic and dynamic constraints imposed by their own embodiment, their environment, and the task at hand. We introduce KinDER, a benchmark for Kinematic and Dynamic Embodied Reasoning that targets physical reasoning challenges arising in robot learning and planning. KinDER comprises 25 procedurally generated environments, a Gymnasium-compatible Python library with parameterized skills and demonstrations, and a standardized evaluation suite with 13 implemented baselines spanning task and motion planning, imitation learning, reinforcement learning, and foundation-model-based approaches. The environments are designed to isolate five core physical reasoning challenges: basic spatial relations, nonprehensile multi-object manipulation, tool use, combinatorial geometric constraints, and dynamic constraints, disentangled from perception, language understanding, and application-specific complexity. Empirical evaluation shows that existing methods struggle to solve many of the environments, indicating substantial gaps in current approaches to physical reasoning. We additionally include real-to-sim-to-real experiments on a mobile manipulator to assess the correspondence between simulation and real-world physical interaction. KinDER is fully open-sourced and intended to enable systematic comparison across diverse paradigms for advancing physical reasoning in robotics. Website and code: https://prpl-group.com/kinder-site/

</details>

### [118] [Measurement of muon (anti-)neutrino charged-current quasielastic-like cross section using off-axis NuMI beam at ICARUS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24925)
*ICARUS Collaboration,F. Abd Alrahman,P. Abratenko,N. Abrego-Martinez,A. Aduszkiewicz,F. Akbar,L. Aliaga Soplin,M. Artero Pons,J. Asaadi,W. F. Badgett,B. Baibussinov,B. Behera,V. Bellini,R. Benocci,J. Berger,S. Bertolucci,M. Betancourt,A. Blanchet,F. Boffelli,M. Bonesini,T. Boone,B. Bottino,A. Braggiotti,D. Brailsford,S. J. Brice,V. Brio,C. Brizzolari,H. S. Budd,A. Campani,A. Campos,D. Carber,M. Carneiro,I. Caro Terrazas,H. Carranza,F. Castillo Fernandez,S. Centro,G. Cerati,A. Chatterjee,D. Cherdack,S. Cherubini,N. Chithirasreemadam,M. Cicerchia,T. E. Coan,A. Cocco,M. R. Convery,L. Cooper-Troendle,S. Copello,H. Da Motta,M. Dallolio,A. A. Dange,A. de Roeck,S. Di Domizio,L. Di Noto,D. Di Ferdinando,M. Diwan,S. Dolan,S. Donati,F. Drielsma,J. Dyer,A. Falcone,C. Farnese,A. Fava,N. Gallice,C. Gatto,D. Gibin,A. Gioiosa,W. Gu,A. Guglielmi,G. Gurung,K. Hassinin,H. Hausner,A. Heggestuen,B. Howard,R. Howell,Z. Hulcher,G. Ingratta,M. S. Ismail,C. James,W. Jang,K. Jung,Y. -J. Jwa,L. Kashur,W. Ketchum,J. S. Kim,D. -H. Koh,J. Larkin,Y. Li,C. Mariani,C. M. Marshall,S. Martynenko,N. Mauri,K. S. McFarland,D. P. Méndez,A. Menegolli,G. Meng,O. G. Miranda,A. Mogan,N. Moggi,E. Montagna,C. Montanari,A. Montanari,M. Mooney,M. Moore,G. Moreno-Granados,J. Mueller,M. Murphy,D. Naples,S. Palestini,M. Pallavicini,V. Paolone,L. Pasqualini,L. Patrizii,G. Petrillo,C. Petta,V. Pia,F. Pietropaolo,F. Poppi,M. Pozzato,M. L. Pumo,G. Putnam,X. Qian,A. Rappoldi,G. L. Raselli,S. Repetto,F. Resnati,A. M. Ricci,M. Rosenberg,M. Rossella,N. Rowe,P. Roy,C. Rubbia,A. Ruggeri,S. Saha,G. Salmoria,S. Samanta,A. Scaramelli,D. Schmitz,A. Schukraft,D. Senadheera,S-H. Seo,F. Sergiampietri,G. Sirri,J. S. Smedley,J. Smith,M. Sotgia,L. Stanco,J. Stewart,H. A. Tanaka,M. Tenti,K. Terao,F. Terranova,V. Togo,D. Torretta,M. Torti,R. Triozzi,Y. -T. Tsai,K. V. Tsang,T. Usher,F. Varanini,N. Vardy,S. Ventura,M. Vicenzi,C. Vignoli,F. A. Wieler,Z. Williams,R. J. Wilson,P. Wilson,J. Wolfs,T. Wongjirad,A. Wood,E. Worcester,M. Worcester,S. Yadav,H. Yu,J. Yu,A. Zani,J. Zennamo,J. Zettlemoyer,S. Zucchelli*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文报道了ICARUS探测器在费米实验室首次利用NuMI中微子束数据测量的中微子截面，聚焦于无π介子产生（CCQE-like）事件的通量平均微分截面，并与多种中微子事件生成器预测进行了比较。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中微子振荡测量的系统不确定性主要来源于复杂的核效应，需要精确的中微子-核相互作用截面测量来约束这些不确定性。

Method: 使用ICARUS探测器采集NuMI束流数据（2.5×10^20 POT），选取末态无π介子产生的事件（CCQE-like拓扑），测量了四个运动学变量的通量平均微分截面：出射轻子角、轻子与领头质子间张角、以及垂直于中微子入射方向的横向平面内μ子和质子的运动学不平衡量。

Result: 获得了四个运动学变量的微分截面测量结果，并与多种中微子事件生成器预测进行了p值比较。总体上预测与数据一致，但当前不确定性预算不足以区分特定模型。

Conclusion: ICARUS首次中微子截面测量结果与现有生成器预测总体吻合，但精度尚不足以对核效应模型进行有效甄别，需进一步积累数据和降低系统不确定性。

Abstract: This paper presents the first neutrino cross-section measurement from the ICARUS detector at Fermilab, using NuMI (Neutrinos at the Main Injector) beam data collected from two beam operation periods corresponding to $2.5\times10^{20}$ protons-on-target in neutrino beam mode. The signal is defined by events with no pions produced in the final state, a topology dominated by charged-current quasi-elastic-like (CCQE-like) signatures. The measurement is reported as flux-averaged differential cross sections as functions of kinematic variables that provide sensitivity to the complex nuclear effects which often dominate the systematic uncertainty budgets of neutrino oscillation measurements. Specifically, this work reports cross sections in two angular variables -- the angle of the outgoing lepton and the opening angle between the lepton and leading proton -- and two variables characterizing the kinematic imbalance between the muon and proton in the plane transverse to the incoming neutrino. These results are compared against predictions from a variety of neutrino event generators, with $p$-values calculated between the extracted cross sections and each prediction. Overall, the predictions agree with the data; however, the current budget of uncertainties does not yet provide sufficient discriminating power to favor a specific model.

</details>


### [119] [Simultaneous measurements of $N$-subjettiness observables in jets from gluons and light-flavour quarks, and in decays of boosted W bosons and top quarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25538)
*CMS Collaboration*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 使用CMS实验在13 TeV质子-质子对撞中收集的138 fb$^{-1}$数据，对大型喷注的25个子结构观测量进行了同时测量，包括双喷注事件和洛伦兹增强的W玻色子/顶夸克衰变事件，测量结果退卷积至稳定粒子层级，并提供了观测量之间的粒子层级相关性估计，用于系统评估和改进喷注中辐射的建模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有喷注子结构测量通常只关注少数几个观测量，缺乏对相空间的完整约束，无法系统评估喷注辐射建模的准确性。本文旨在通过大量子结构观测量的同时测量，提供更全面的喷注内部辐射特征刻画。

Method: 在质子-质子对撞中选取高横动量大半径喷注，利用双喷注和$\mathrm{t\bar{t}}$事件样本分别对应1、2、3个硬子结构（prong）的喷注；使用基于$N$-subjettiness的6体基组（25个观测量）对喷注中已解析辐射的相空间进行过约束测量，并退卷积至稳定粒子层级。

Result: 获得了25个子结构观测量在三种不同喷注类型（单支、双支、三支）中的同时测量结果，以及粒子层级观测量之间的相关性矩阵估计。

Conclusion: 该测量为系统评估和改进蒙特卡罗事件生成器中喷注辐射建模提供了丰富的实验约束，退卷积后的结果可直接与粒子级理论预测进行比较。

Abstract: A simultaneous measurement of 25 substructure observables is presented using large-radius jets with high transverse momentum from proton-proton collisions at $\sqrt{s}$ = 13 TeV. The measurement is carried out on dijet events and $\mathrm{t\bar{t}}$ events enriched in Lorentz-boosted W bosons and top quarks decaying hadronically. The three data samples consist of jets with one, two, or three prongs from the showering and hadronization of a gluon or light-flavour quark, two quarks, or three quarks, respectively. The data correspond to an integrated luminosity of 138 fb$^{-1}$, recorded by the CMS experiment in 2016$-$2018. A detailed characterization of the jet substructure is provided using a 6-body basis of $N$-subjettiness observables that overconstrains the phase space of the resolved emissions in the jet. The measurements are unfolded to the level of stable particles, and an estimate of the particle-level correlations between observables is provided, ensuring that the results can be used to systematically assess and refine the modelling of radiation in jets.

</details>


### [120] [Measurement of the Z $\to$ $μ^+μ^-$ angular coefficients in pp collisions at $\sqrt{s}$ = 13 TeV as functions of transverse momentum and rapidity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25678)
*CMS Collaboration*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 基于CMS探测器在13 TeV质子-质子对撞中收集的140 fb⁻¹数据，测量了Drell-Yan过程产生的双缪子末态的八个角极化系数A₀-A₇，结果在双缪子横动量和快度的多个区间内呈双微分分布，并与NNLO QCD理论预言进行了比较。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通过精确测量Drell-Yan过程的角极化系数，探究Z玻色子产生机制及部分子层次的动力学信息，检验微扰量子色动力学的理论预言。

Method: 利用CMS探测器在√s=13 TeV质子-质子对撞中收集的140 fb⁻¹数据，在双缪子不变质量区间81-101 GeV内，对八个角极化系数A₀-A₇进行双微分测量，分为8个横动量区间和2个快度区间。

Result: 得到了A₀-A₇八个角极化系数在横动量和快度空间中的双微分分布测量值，并与NNLO pQCD理论计算进行了比较。

Conclusion: 该测量提供了关于Z玻色子产生机制和部分子动力学的重要信息，对理解Drell-Yan过程和检验高阶QCD理论具有重要价值。

Abstract: A measurement of the eight angular polarization coefficients, $A_0$ to $A_7$, in the cross section for the Drell$-$Yan production of two muons is presented. The analysis is based on proton-proton (pp) collision data recorded with the CMS detector at the LHC at a center-of-mass energy of $\sqrt{s}$ = 13 TeV, corresponding to an integrated luminosity of 140 fb$^{-1}$. The coefficients are determined double differentially in eight intervals of transverse momentum and two intervals of rapidity of the muon pair $μ^+μ^-$. The results are presented for the $μ^+μ^-$ invariant mass range 81$-$101 GeV and are compared with theoretical predictions calculated at next-to-next-to-leading order in perturbative quantum chromodynamics. The measurement provides relevant information about the underlying partonic dynamics and the Z boson production mechanisms.

</details>


### [121] [Sub-keV energy calibration of CONUS+ via 71Ge M-shell neutron activation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25748)
*E. Sánchez García,Y. Shi,N. Ackermann,H. Bonet,C. Buck,J. Hakenmüller,G. Heusser,M. Lindner,W. Maneschg,M. Meier,S. Mertens,D. Piani,T. Rink,H. Strecker*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: CONUS+实验通过中子活化标定，将反应堆CEvNS测量中能量刻度的不确定度从14%降低到4%以下，并首次清晰分辨出71Ge的M壳层X射线线（158.7±1.4 eVee），验证了能量重建到探测阈值的准确性，为精确CEvNS测量和新物理搜索奠定了基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CONUS+实验首次探测到反应堆反中微子在锗核上的相干弹性中微子-核散射（CEvNS），现已进入精确测量阶段。首次测量中最大的系统不确定度来自能量刻度（对信号预测贡献14%的不确定度），因此需要更精确的刻度方法以降低误差，支撑后续的精确测量和超出标准模型的新物理搜索。

Method: 使用强241AmBe中子源对CONUS+实验中一个2.4 kg的锗探测器进行专门的中子活化辐照，通过分析活化产物的X射线能谱来标定能量响应，验证能量刻度、能量分辨率、触发效率以及物理信号与噪声事件的分离能力。

Result: 成功将能量刻度对信号预测的不确定度贡献从14%降低到4%以下；首次清晰分辨出71Ge的M壳层X射线线，能量为(158.7±1.4) eVee，验证了CONUS+探测器在探测阈值附近的能量重建能力。

Conclusion: 该中子活化标定方法为CONUS+实验的精确能量刻度奠定了基础，计划未来在Leibstadt核电站现场开展进一步的活化标定实验，从而增强CONUS+对精确CEvNS测量和超出标准模型物理的灵敏度。

Abstract: The CONUS+ experiment has recently reported the first detection of coherent elastic neutrino-nucleus scattering (CEvNS) of reactor antineutrinos on germanium nuclei and is now entering a precision phase. The dominant uncertainty in the first measurement was the energy scale, which contributed 14% to the uncertainty of the prediction of the combined signal. We present a dedicated neutron activation campaign in which one of the new 2.4 kg CONUS+ germanium detectors was irradiated with a strong 241AmBe source, demonstrating that a contribution below 4% to the uncertainty of signal prediction is achievable. For the first time, the 71Ge M-shell X-ray line was clearly resolved at (158.7+-1.4) eVee, validating the CONUS+ energy reconstruction down to the detection threshold. This validation includes the understanding of the energy scale, the energy resolution, the trigger efficiency, and the correct separation of physical from noise events. These results establish the foundation for a future activation campaign at the Kernkraftwerk Leibstadt reactor site, strengthening the CONUS+ energy calibration and extending its sensitivity to precision CEvNS and beyond Standard Model physics measurements.

</details>


<div id='math.NT'></div>

# math.NT [[Back]](#toc)

### [122] [Ramanujan, the taxicab problem for polynomials, and the abc-conjecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25017)
*Katalin Gyarmati*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文从拉马努金的出租车问题出发，研究多项式方程 $p^n+q^n=r^n+s^n$ 以及更一般的 $p_1^{k_1}+\dots+p_m^{k_m}=0$ 的可解性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受拉马努金出租车问题的启发，探索多项式情形下类似丢番图方程的可解性问题。

Method: 通过代数与数论方法，分析多项式指数和方程的解的存在性与构造。

Result: 给出了多项式情形下 $p^n+q^n=r^n+s^n$ 及更一般形式方程可解性的条件或构造。

Conclusion: 将经典的整数丢番图问题推广到多项式环中，丰富了相关理论。

Abstract: Starting with Ramanujan's famous taxicab problem, we can study the solvability of the equations $p^n+q^n=r^n+s^n$ and, more generally, $p_1^{k_1}+\dots+p_m^{k_m}=0$ among polynomials.

</details>


### [123] [Linear recurrences and rational Lambert series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25151)
*Igor Rivin*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文证明了一个刚性定理：对于最终线性递归序列γ，如果其Lambert级数L_γ(z)是有理函数，则γ只有有限个非零项。等价地，在有理普通生成函数的序列中，只有有限支撑序列的Lambert级数是有理函数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 刻画具有有理Lambert级数的序列特征，建立序列支撑性质与生成函数有理性的联系。

Method: 将问题数据特化到有限生成环的有限位上，利用有限域上递归序列的周期性进行证明。

Result: 最终线性递归序列γ的Lambert级数为有理函数当且仅当γ是有限支撑的。

Conclusion: 揭示了Lambert级数有理性对序列支撑的强约束，即有限支撑是唯一可能的情形。

Abstract: For a sequence $γ=(γ_n)_{n\ge 1}$, define \[ L_γ(z):=\sum_{n\ge 1}γ_n\frac{z^n}{1-z^n} =\sum_{n\ge 1}\Bigl(\sum_{d\mid n}γ_d\Bigr)z^n. \] We prove a short rigidity theorem: if $γ$ is eventually linearly recurrent and $L_γ(z)$ is rational, then $γ$ is finitely supported. Equivalently, among sequences with rational ordinary generating function, the only ones whose Lambert series is rational are the finitely supported sequences. The proof specializes the data at a finite place of a finitely generated ring and then uses the periodicity of recurrences over finite fields.

</details>


### [124] [Trilinear Kloosterman fractions I: partially fixed moduli and unbalanced convolutions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25177)
*Thomas Wright*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文改进了Fouvry和Radziwiłł关于非平衡卷积的结果，在更宽的参数范围内建立了卷积和与平均值的误差估计，同时改进了Bettin和Chandee关于含Kloosterman分式三线性形式的著名结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在解析数论中，非平衡卷积的估计对于理解模算子的分布性质至关重要。现有结果在参数N和Q的允许范围上存在局限，需要进一步优化以应用于更广泛的数论问题。

Method: 通过改进Bettin和Chandee关于分母具有固定因子时Kloosterman分式三线性形式的估计，结合对序列β在小模数下均匀分布性质的分析，推导出更优的误差界。

Result: 在Q≤X^{1/2+1/66-δ}条件下，将N的容许范围扩展到exp((log x)^ε) ≤ N ≤ Q^{-11/12} X^{17/36-ε}；在Q≤X^{45/89-ε}条件下给出更宽的N范围，同时改进了Q的容许范围。

Conclusion: 本文在非平衡卷积的误差估计中取得了参数范围的双重改进，为相关数论问题的研究提供了更强大的工具。

Abstract: In this paper, we improve on Fouvry and Radziwiłł's results on unbalanced convolutions. In particular, we find that if $(α_m)$ and $(β_n)$ are sequences supported on $m\sim M$ and $n\sim M$ where $β$ is equidistributed for small moduli, then \begin{gather*}\sum_{q\sim Q}\left|\mathop{\sum\sum}_{\substack{n\sim N,m\sim M \\ mn\equiv a\pmod q}}α_mβ_n-\frac{1}{φ(q)}\mathop{\sum\sum}_{\substack{n\sim N,m\sim M \\ (mn,q)=1}}α_mβ_n\right|\ll \frac{X}{\log^A X}, \end{gather*} as long as $\exp((\log x)^{\varepsilon}) \leq N \leq Q^{-11/12} X^{17/36-\varepsilon}$ with $Q\leq X^{1/2+1/66-δ}$, along with wider bounds for $N$ if $Q\leq X^{\frac{45}{89}-ε}$. The former improves the allowable range of $N$, while the latter improves the range of $Q$. To prove these new bounds, we improve Bettin and Chandee's famous result on trilinear forms with Kloosterman fractions in the case where the denominator has a fixed factor.

</details>


### [125] [Reciprocity and the Maslov Phase](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25288)
*Jonathan Holland*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文通过元保角（metaplectic）方法给出了希尔伯特互反律的一种新证明，进而推导了二次互反律。其中，局部相位由Kashiwara-Maslov三元拉格朗日子空间相位给出。在秩二情形，有序三元组$(L_\infty,L_a,L_0)$的相位对应一维Weil指标$\gamma_v(a)$。局部Hilbert符号表现为这些相位的严格可乘性缺陷：$(a,b)_v = \frac{\gamma_v(a)\gamma_v(b)}{\gamma_v(1)\gamma_v(ab)}$。全局步骤通过比较单个Bruhat词在局部与adelic实现来完成。对于有理adelic数据，归一化后的Bruhat词具有可乘性。互反律表明总缺陷$\prod_v\mu_v(a,b)$为1，结合局部桥梁即得Hilbert互反律，而二次互反律是其在奇素数对$(p,q)$情形下的特例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为希尔伯特互反律和二次互反律提供一种新的元保角（metaplectic）证明，揭示其与Kashiwara-Maslov相位及Weil指标之间的内在联系。

Method: 利用元保角群表示理论，将局部相位定义为Kashiwara-Maslov三元拉格朗日子空间相位，建立局部Hilbert符号与一维Weil指标$\gamma_v(a)$之间的桥梁；通过Bruhat词在局部与adelic实现中的比较，定义缺陷函数$\mu_v(a,b)$，并证明全局积为1。

Result: 建立了局部Hilbert符号与Weil指标缺陷之间的等式关系：$(a,b)_v = \frac{\gamma_v(a)\gamma_v(b)}{\gamma_v(1)\gamma_v(ab)}$；证明了全局缺陷积$\prod_v\mu_v(a,b)=1$，从而推导出希尔伯特互反律和二次互反律。

Conclusion: 通过元保角方法统一了局部和全局互反律的证明框架，揭示了互反律与元保角表示、Maslov相位之间的深层结构联系。

Abstract: We give a metaplectic proof of Hilbert reciprocity, and hence of quadratic reciprocity, in which the local phase is the Kashiwara--Maslov phase of a triple of Lagrangians. In rank two the phase of the ordered triple $(L_\infty,L_a,L_0)$ is the one-dimensional Weil index $γ_v(a)$. The local Hilbert symbol appears as the defect of strict multiplicativity of these phases: \[ (a,b)_v = \frac{γ_v(a)γ_v(b)}{γ_v(1)γ_v(ab)}. \] The global step compares the local and adelic realizations of a single Bruhat word for the diagonal torus elements $m(a)\in \operatorname{SL}_2(\mathbb Q)$. Locally the raw Bruhat-word lift carries the normalization factor determined by the chosen quadratic convention. These operators form a projective representation of the diagonal torus with defect \[ μ_v(a,b) = \frac{γ_v(a)γ_v(b)}{γ_v(1)γ_v(ab)}. \] For rational adelic data, the normalized Bruhat word is multiplicative. The reciprocity law states that the total defect $\prod_vμ_v(a,b)$ is $1$. Combined with the local bridge above, this yields Hilbert reciprocity, while quadratic reciprocity is then the specialization to the pair of odd primes $(p,q)$.

</details>


### [126] [Congruences via Partitions with Exactly Two Part Sizes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25394)
*Sittinon Jirattikansakul,Teeradej Kittipassorn,Kraiwich Kongsiri,Nitipon Moonwichit,Kirati Sriamorn*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文证明了对于特定形式的N（N = An + B，其中(A,B)属于五组特定值），和式∑_{1≤k<√N} σ₀(N - k²) ≡ 0 (mod 4)，其中σ₀(m)表示m的正除数个数。证明依赖于Keith关于ν₂(N)（N的恰有两个部分大小的分拆数）满足ν₂(N) ≡ 0 (mod 4)的结果，并结合了组合论证与模算术技巧。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究特定数论函数（除数函数与分拆函数）之间的同余关系，探索分拆数ν₂(N)的模4性质如何影响除数函数求和式的模4性质。

Method: 利用Keith关于ν₂(N) ≡ 0 (mod 4)的定理，通过组合论证与模算术技巧，将∑_{1≤k<√N} σ₀(N - k²)的模4性质规约到ν₂(N)的模4性质上，对五类特定形式的N进行验证。

Result: 成功证明了对于N = An + B，其中(A,B) ∈ {(16,14), (36,30), (72,42), (196,70), (252,114)}，有∑_{1≤k<√N} σ₀(N - k²) ≡ 0 (mod 4)。

Conclusion: 建立了除数函数求和式与恰有两个部分大小的分拆数之间的同余联系，推广了Dewitt和Keith的相关工作。

Abstract: We prove the congruence $\sum_{1 \leq k < \sqrt{N}} σ_0 (N - k^2) \equiv 0 \pmod 4$, where $σ_0(m)$ denotes the number of positive divisors of $m$, for $N = An + B$ with $(A,B) \in \{ (16,14),$ $(36,30),$ $(72,42),$ $(196,70),$ $(252,114) \}$. Our proof relies on a result of Keith which states that $ν_2 (N) \equiv 0 \pmod 4$, where $ν_2(N)$ is the number of partitions of $N$ with exactly two part sizes. Inspired by Dewitt and Keith, our approach combines combinatorial arguments with modular arithmetic techniques.

</details>


### [127] [Additive index and Carlitz rank](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25414)
*Pierre-Yves Bienvenu,Arne Winterhof*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文比较了有限域自映射的几种复杂度度量，证明Carlitz秩与加法指数不能同时很小（除平凡情况外），表明两种度量检测不同函数类的密码学弱点。同时研究了加法指数与次数/重量之间的关系，补充了前人关于Carlitz秩与次数/重量关系的结果。最后证明离散对数相关函数使得所有四种复杂度度量（次数、重量、加法指数、Carlitz秩）都很大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了更全面地理解有限域上函数的密码学强度，需要比较不同复杂度度量之间的关系，探究它们是否能同时保持较小值，从而发现不同函数类可能存在的密码学弱点。

Method: 通过理论分析与对比研究，考察Carlitz秩、加法指数、次数和重量四种复杂度度量之间的相互约束关系，寻找满足或不满足同时小复杂度条件的函数实例。

Result: 1) Carlitz秩和加法指数除平凡情况外不能同时很小，说明两者检测不同函数类的密码学弱点；2) 补充了加法指数与次数/重量关系的结果；3) 离散对数相关函数使所有四种复杂度度量都很大。

Conclusion: 不同复杂度度量捕捉函数的不同密码学性质，需要综合评估函数的密码学强度；离散对数相关函数在所有考虑复杂度度量下均表现良好，是密码学上强的函数。

Abstract: We compare several complexity measures for self-mappings of finite fields. In particular, we show that Carlitz rank and additive index cannot be small simultaneously up to trivial exceptions. That is, these two measures detect cryptographic weaknesses of different classes of functions. We also study the relationship between additive index and degree or weight, respectively, complementing earlier results of Aksoy et al. and Gómez-Pérez et al. on the relationship between Carlitz rank and degree or weight, respectively. Finally, we show that a function closely related to the discrete logarithm provides an example in which all four complexity measures, degree, weight, additive index and Carlitz rank, are large.

</details>


### [128] [The Divisor Function along a Deterministic Orbit and the Emergence of Ladders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25446)
*Marco Mantovanelli*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究了确定性递归 $n_{j+1} = n_j - τ(n_j)$ 及其轨道长度 $a(x)$，通过建立结构-随机性原理，将轨道分析简化为除数函数的二尺度分布问题，并在反集中假设下证明了 $a(x) \asymp x / \log x$。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解除数函数 $τ(n)$ 的确定性递归轨道行为，尤其是轨道长度 $a(x)$ 的渐近增长，该问题由于沿轨道的强依赖性而超出传统乘性函数方法的研究范围。

Method: 建立了一个确定性框架，将轨道分析约化为 $τ(n_j)$ 在二尺度上的分布问题；提出了结构-随机性原理，区分除数混合与强加法结构两种情形；在后一种情形下，引入相位刚性假设，证明了长近等差序列（除数阶梯）的存在性。

Result: 在反集中假设（排除能量饱和除数阶梯）下，得到 $a(x) \asymp x / \log x$；将 $a(x)$ 的渐近行为归结为单一结构障碍；无条件证明了 $τ(n)$ 的大值在二尺度上可忽略，且潜在障碍只能出现在单一除数尺度 $τ(n) \asymp \log n$。

Conclusion: 轨道长度的渐近分析本质上被约化为一个结构性障碍（除数阶梯）是否存在的问题，为完全解决该问题指明了方向。

Abstract: We study the deterministic recursion $n_{j+1} = n_j - τ(n_j)$, where $τ(n)$ denotes the divisor function, and the associated orbit length $a(x)$. Heuristics based on the average order of $τ(n)$ suggest that $a(x) \asymp x / \log x$, but the strong dependence along the orbit places the problem outside the scope of existing methods for multiplicative functions. We develop a deterministic framework that reduces the analysis of the orbit to the distribution of $τ(n_j)$ on dyadic scales. This yields a structure-versus-randomness principle: either the orbit exhibits divisor mixing, or it develops strong additive structure. In the latter case, we show, under a phase-rigidity hypothesis, that the orbit contains long near-arithmetic progressions along which $τ(n)$ is essentially constant, which we call divisor ladders. Our main result reduces the asymptotic behavior of $a(x)$ to a single structural obstruction. Assuming an anti-concentration hypothesis that rules out energy-saturating divisor ladders, we obtain $a(x) \asymp x / \log x$. The paper also establishes several unconditional structural results, including that large values of $τ(n)$ are negligible on dyadic scales and that any potential obstruction must occur at a single divisor scale $τ(n) \asymp \log n$.

</details>


### [129] [The threshold for linear independence of multiple zeta values in positive characteristic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25451)
*Bo-Hae Im,Hojin Kim,Tuan Ngo Dac*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文解决了Thakur在2009年提出的关于正特征下多重zeta值（MZV）的$\mathbb{F}_q$-线性无关性的基本猜想。证明了权重不超过2q时MZV保持线性无关，而在权重2q+1时存在唯一且显式的$\mathbb{F}_q$-线性关系，首次给出了该猜想的反例。证明基于MZV与Carlitz多重对数的新联系，并引入了一种保持权重的算子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Thakur在2009年提出的猜想认为正特征下固定权重的MZV在$\mathbb{F}_q$上线性无关，这一猜想指导了函数域算术领域的重大发展。本文旨在确定这一无关性的精确阈值。

Method: 1. 建立了MZV与Carlitz多重对数在$\mathbb{F}_q$上的新联系，推广了[IKLNDP24]的核心结果；2. 修改了[ND21]中的算法，引入了一个保持权重的算子，作用于$\mathbb{F}_q$-线性关系，构成了代数框架。

Result: 权重不超过2q时线性无关性成立；权重2q+1时存在唯一且显式的$\mathbb{F}_q$-线性关系，即首次给出了Thakur猜想的反例。

Conclusion: 完全解决了Thakur猜想，确定了线性无关性的精确阈值，并提供了第一个反例，深化了对函数域算术中MZV结构的理解。

Abstract: A fundamental conjecture formulated by Thakur in 2009, which has guided significant developments in function field arithmetic, asserts that multiple zeta values (MZV's) in positive characteristic of fixed weight are linearly independent over $\mathbb{F}_q$. In this paper we settle this conjecture by determining the precise threshold for this independence. We prove that linear independence holds for all weights up to 2q, while for weight 2q+1 we establish the existence of a unique and explicit $\mathbb{F}_q$-linear relation. This result provides the first counterexample to Thakur's conjecture. Our proof relies on a new connection between MZVs and Carlitz multiple polylogarithms over $\mathbb{F}_q$, generalizing a central result of [IKLNDP24]. We also introduce a modification of the algorithm from [ND21] that yields a weight-preserving operator acting on $\mathbb{F}_q$-linear relations, providing the algebraic framework for these results.

</details>


### [130] [Cyclotomic Numbers of Order $q-1$ over $\mathbb{F}_{q^r}$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25461)
*Hayaki Kudo,Yuto Nogata*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究了有限域 $\mathbb{F}_{q^r}$ 上的分圆数 $(a,b)_{q-1}$，证明了当 $q\neq 2$ 或 $r<3$ 时，分圆数满足上界 $\left\lceil \frac{k}{2}\right\rceil$，其中 $k=\frac{q^r-1}{q-1}$；并对素数 $r$（特别是 $r=2$ 和 $r=3$）给出了更紧的界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分圆数是数论和有限域理论中的基本研究对象，在编码理论、密码学等领域有重要应用。给出分圆数的上界有助于理解其分布特性，为相关应用提供理论依据。

Method: 通过代数数论和组合方法，对分圆数 $(a,b)_{q-1}$ 进行估值分析，利用指数和、特征和等工具推导上界，并针对素数 $r$ 进行特殊处理以得到更优的界。

Result: 证明了对于所有 $0\le a,b\le q-2$，分圆数 $(a,b)_{q-1}\le \left\lceil \frac{k}{2}\right\rceil$ 在 $q\neq 2$ 或 $r<3$ 时成立；对 $r=2$ 和 $r=3$ 等素数情形给出了更紧的界。

Conclusion: 建立了有限域分圆数的一般性上界，并针对特殊参数给出了更精确的估计，丰富了分圆数的理论研究。

Abstract: Let $q=p^n$, $r\in \mathbb{Z}_{\ge 2}$, $e=q-1$, and $k=\frac{q^r-1}{e}$. In this paper, we study the cyclotomic numbers $(a,b)_{q-1}$ over $\mathbb{F}_{q^r}$. We prove that $(a,b)_{q-1}\le \left\lceil \frac{k}{2}\right\rceil$ for all $0\le a,b\le q-2$ except when $q=2$ and $r\ge 3$. We also give sharper bounds for prime values of $r$, especially for $r=2$ and $r=3$.

</details>


### [131] [Some results on naive transcendence in the ring of integers modulo infinitely large primes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25566)
*Toshiki Matsusaka,Shin-ichiro Seki*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文在整数模无穷大素数环 $\mathcal{A}$ 中研究超越性，区分了Rosen提出的有限代数数超越性与朴素超越性，改进了已有结果并给出朴素超越数的新例子，同时在ABC猜想下证明了某些数的无理性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深入理解环 $\mathcal{A}$ 中两种超越性概念（有限代数数超越性与朴素超越性）之间的关系，改进并推广Anzawa-Funakura和Luca-Zudilin的现有结果。

Method: 通过去除已有证明中的部分假设条件，将部分结果从有限代数数超越性升级为朴素超越性；利用ABC猜想证明特定数的无理性。

Result: 改进了Anzawa-Funakura和Luca-Zudilin的结果，去除了部分假设；给出了若干朴素超越数的新例子；在ABC猜想下证明了 $\log_{\mathcal{A}}(2)$ 等数的无理性。

Conclusion: 环 $\mathcal{A}$ 中两种超越性概念具有层次关系，朴素超越性蕴含有限代数数超越性；通过改进条件可获得更强的超越性结论；部分数的朴素超越性仍需进一步研究，但可借助ABC猜想获得无理性结果。

Abstract: This paper presents various transcendence results in the ring of integers modulo infinitely large primes $\mathcal{A}$. In the ring $\mathcal{A}$, one can consider two notions of transcendence. One is based on the notion of finite algebraic numbers introduced by Rosen, while the other is transcendence in the naive sense. It is known that transcendence in the latter sense automatically implies transcendence in the former sense. In this paper, we strengthen results of Anzawa-Funakura and Luca-Zudilin by removing some of their assumptions and, in some cases, upgrading them to statements of naive transcendence. We also present several examples of naive transcendental numbers that do not seem to have appeared previously in the literature. Although we are not able to establish naive transcendence for certain numbers, we prove the irrationality of numbers such as $\log_{\mathcal{A}}(2)$ under the ABC conjecture.

</details>


### [132] [Conditional Upper Bounds for Large Deviations and Moments of the Riemann Zeta Function](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25579)
*Louis-Pierre Arguin,Emma Bailey,Asher Roberts*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 在黎曼猜想下，本文证明了zeta函数在临界线上大值的测度上界，并恢复了Harper关于2k阶矩的界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究黎曼zeta函数在临界线上的大值概率及高阶矩的精确上界。

Method: 基于作者与Bourgade、Radziwill的递归方案，结合Soundararajan和Harper的思想。

Result: 对k>0，大值测度上界为C_k (log T)^{-k^2}/sqrt(log log T)，其中C_k=exp(e^{ck})；2k阶矩上界为C_k (log T)^{k^2}。

Conclusion: 在黎曼猜想下给出了zeta函数大值概率和矩的改进上界。

Abstract: Assuming the Riemann Hypothesis, we show that for $k>0$ $$ \frac{1}{T}\text{meas}\Big\{t\in [T,2T]:|ζ(1/2+{\rm i} t)|>(\log T)^k\Big\}\leq C_k \frac{(\log T)^{-k^2}}{\sqrt{\log\log T}}, $$ where $C_k=\exp(e^{ck})$ for some absolute constant $c>0$. This implies that the $2k$-moments of $|ζ|$ are bounded above by $C_k(\log T)^{k^2}$, recovering the bound of Harper. The proof relies on the recursive scheme of one of the authors with Bourgade and Radziwill (2020), and combines ideas of Soundararajan (2009) and Harper (2013).

</details>


### [133] [On numerical semigroups with embedding dimension four](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25653)
*Kazimierz Chomicz*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种几何方法，用于寻找任意嵌入维数为四的数值半群的Apéry集，并利用该方法计算了由四个连续平方数和四个连续三角形数生成的数值半群的Frobenius数、亏格、Betti元素、最小表示和链环度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在处理嵌入维数为四的数值半群时存在局限性，需要一种更通用的几何方法来系统计算其Apéry集及相关不变量。

Method: 开发了一种几何程序来构造嵌入维数为四的数值半群的Apéry集，并将其应用于两类具体的数值半群（四个连续平方数和四个连续三角形数生成）。

Result: 成功计算了由四个连续平方数和四个连续三角形数生成的数值半群的Frobenius数、亏格、Betti元素、最小表示和链环度。

Conclusion: 该几何方法有效简化了嵌入维数为四的数值半群的结构分析，并得出了两类特殊半群的具体不变量。

Abstract: We develop a geometric procedure for finding the Apéry set of any numerical semigroup with embedding dimension four. We use this method to find the Frobenius numbers, genera, Betti elements, minimal presentations and catenary degrees of numerical semigroups generated by four consecutive squares and four consecutive triangular numbers.

</details>


### [134] [A structure theorem for sets with doubling $4+δ$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25893)
*Yifan Jing,Akshat Mudgal*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文证明了整数集合在倍率至多为4+δ（δ>0充分小）时的结构结果，推广了Eberhard-Green-Manners关于倍率严格小于4的工作，推进了Green提出的一个问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推进Green提出的关于整数集合倍率接近4时的结构性问题，将已有结果从严格小于4推广到4+δ的范围。

Method: 通过分析整数集合的加倍结构，利用组合数学和加法数论的工具，对倍率在4到4+δ之间的情况进行结构性刻画。

Result: 得到了当δ充分小时，倍率不超过4+δ的整数集合的结构性描述，推广了此前仅适用于倍率严格小于4的结果。

Conclusion: 本文在加法数论中关于整数集合结构理论方面取得了进展，部分解决了Green提出的问题，为理解倍率接近4的集合结构提供了新见解。

Abstract: We prove a structural result for sets of integers with doubling at most $4 + δ$, with $δ>0$ sufficiently small. This generalises earlier work of Eberhard--Green--Manners which dealt with sets of integers with doubling strictly less than $4$, and makes progress towards a question of Green.

</details>
